Same for names missing from liquidity_lookup. The gate therefore does not restore fidelity for the two fail-closed classes it advertises; it creates a new (conservative-direction) divergence. (2) The 'fail-closed on unknown live-account list' guarantee holds only on the exception path: I ran cap_lag_orders(..., live_labels=[]) and got qty 50,000 → 20,000 with share=1.0, no block. With one live account on a non-DNSE broker (live_dnse_labels filters broker=='dnse' at config.py:206, pick_accounts does not), each account enforces the full 20% → 40% ADV, exactly the bug cap_capit_orders' docstring claims to have fixed. (3) Minor: min_fill_pct=0.30 is the engine default at simulate_holistic_nav.py:354, not part of LIQ_LAG at pt_v23_audit_2014.py:1135 as cited. (4) Minor: paper accounts get share=1.0 vs live 1/N, so live under-fills the paper mirror it diffs against on thin names — unlisted tracking error.", "": ""}, "independent_recompute": "bq query on tav2_bq.ticker for IVS/TMG/TRC 2026-07-15..21: IVS 28,200×6,400 = 1.8048E8 (finding: 0.180 tỷ ✓, cap 36tr ✓); TRC 18,350×76,000 = 1.3946E9 on 2026-07-20 (finding: 1.395 tỷ ✓, cap 279tr / 139tr ✓); TMG Volume_3M_P50 = 0.0 on 2026-07-17 ✓. Also re-ran lag_adv_cap_selfcheck.py (22/22 PASS) and directly probed cap_lag_orders with live_labels=[] (fail-open reproduced). All quoted numbers matched to the stated precision.", "killer_objection": "The gate's own justification misreads the simulator: simulate_holistic_nav.py treats liq==0 or a missing lookup as NO CAP (full fill), so TMG — the headline example — is bought at full size in the pinned R3 backtest, not 'contributes 0'. Blocking it live is a NEW live-vs-backtest divergence, not the closing of one; and the companion 'fail-closed' guarantee is fail-OPEN whenever live_dnse_labels() returns an empty or non-matching list, reproducing the 2×20% = 40% ADV bug the design explicitly claims to prevent.", "recommended_reruns": ["Fix fail-open: block (or hard-raise) when live_labels is empty, or when the executing account is mode==live but absent from live_labels — do not silently fall back to share=1.0. Add a self-check case for live_labels=[] and for a live account missing from a non-empty list.", "Restate the TMG/missing-ADV rationale honestly: 'stricter than the backtest by design (engine leaves liq<=0 uncapped, simulate_holistic_nav.py:1169-1172)' rather than 'closes a fidelity gap'. Quantify how many LAG deals in the pinned R3 run were filled on liq<=0 or missing-lookup days — if non-trivial, the live gate materially under-trades vs the 27.84% baseline and that must be re-measured, not assumed.", "Fix the citation: min_fill_pct=0.30 is the simulate() default (simulate_holistic_nav.py:354), not a key of LIQ_LAG at pt_v23_audit_2014.py:1135.", "Decide the paper-vs-live share asymmetry: paper share=1.0 vs live 1/N makes the mirror the live target diffs against unreachable on thin names — either apply the same share to paper mirrors or document the resulting drift.", "Consider deriving share from the account's live NAV (pro-rata, as cap_capit_orders does) rather than 1/N, or state why equal-split under-allocation on the larger account is acceptable."], "summary": "The code is real, runnable, correctly wired, and its arithmetic checks out: I re-ran the self-check (22/22 PASS) and independently re-queried BigQuery — every ADV and cap number in the payload matches exactly, round_lot rounds down so trims never exceed the cap, there is no look-ahead, the carry-over claim is verified in strategies.py/executor.py, and 0.20 is copied from the backtest spec rather than tuned. Two claims nonetheless fail. First, the stated mechanism is wrong: simulate_holistic_nav.py:1169-1172 leaves daily_max uncapped when the liquidity lookup is zero or missing, so TMG is filled at full size in the pinned R3 backtest — blocking it live is a new divergence in the safe direction, not the closing of a fidelity gap the finding claims. Second, the advertised fail-closed guarantee on 'không xác định được danh sách account live' is fail-OPEN: an empty or non-matching live_labels list silently yields share=1.0 per account, and since live_dnse_labels filters broker=='dnse' while pick_accounts does not, a future non-DNSE live account reproduces the 40%-ADV bug the design says it fixed. Because this is a hard gate on real SpaceX/ZaloPay orders, it is not production-ready as claimed; the two fixes are small and after adding an empty-live-list self-check case this should re-verify cleanly."}

The gate is therefore a NEW conservative divergence, exactly as the v2 docstring now says. (b) The min_fill_pct=0.30 citation is now correct (simulate_holistic_nav.py:354 default, not a LIQ_LAG key). (c) share = 1/N over all-broker live accounts; my probe confirms 2 accounts → 0.5 each → total exactly 20% ADV, no 40% doubling. (d) ticker_prune 2014+ re-queried: 738,201 rows, 0 with liq<=0 — confirms the issue is confined to the non-prune LAG universe. Result: 5,319 events / 790 tickers (payload: 5.319 / 790, EXACT match); liq<=0 = 683 = 12.84% (payload 681 = 12.80%); NaN column = 83 = 1.56% (payload 83 = 1.56%, EXACT); no-row-on-entry = 5 vs their 24; total blocked 14.50% vs 14.81%. The A-bucket gap is a trading-calendar source difference (my calendar runs to 2026-07-20 vs their END_DATE). Separately re-queried BQ: ticker_prune 2014+ has 738,201 rows and 0 with Volume_3M_P50*Close <= 0 or NULL, confirming their '0 dòng liq<=0' claim. Also directly probed cap_lag_orders' four share/mode branches. Everything matched to the quoted precision.", "killer_objection": "The '14,8% là CẬN TRÊN theo event' framing is unsupported and the mechanism points the other way. Cash rationing (LAG ~6x oversubscribed, binds 92% of entries) does not select funded deals independently of liquidity: in the engine a liq==0 name is UNCAPPED, so it completes its whole target in a single session and exits the fill queue, while measurable-ADV names are throttled to 20%/day and occupy capital for up to max_fill_days=5. That makes liq=0 names systematically OVER-represented among actually-funded deals, so the live under-trade versus pinned R3 could exceed 14.8%, not fall below it. Since this is the exact number the user is being asked to accept when approving Phương án A, presenting it as an upper bound understates the risk being signed off. It does not void the code fix — it voids the reassurance attached to it.", "recommended_reruns": ["Do not quote 14,8% as an upper bound. Measure the DEAL-level (funded) share directly: instrument the pinned R3 pt_v23 run to log, per executed LAG fill, whether liquidity_lookup.get((tk, day)) was missing or <= 0, then report blocked-fill-value / total-LAG-fill-value. That is the number that actually maps to live under-trade.", "Execute the Phương án A re-pin the payload already scopes: rerun pt_v23 with the engine treating liq<=0 / missing-key as BLOCK (change `if liq and liq > 0` to an explicit block branch) and compare CAGR/Sharpe/Calmar against 27.84%. Until that exists, no pinned number describes the post-gate live path.", "Fix the cosmetic count: the self-check prints 29 ✅ and has no counter — either add an explicit PASS/TOTAL counter or stop quoting '27/27' in payloads.", "Decide the max_fill_days=5 / min_fill_pct=0.30 abandon rule separately (correctly deferred, not invented here) — direction of drift is backtest-gives-up vs live-keeps-buying, which compounds with this gate's under-trade in the opposite direction.", "Consider whether the paper branch with live_labels==[] → share=1.0 should also block, for symmetry; low priority since paper consumes no real liquidity, and the self-check asserts it as intended behaviour."], "summary": "This is a genuine, verifiable correction of the version I refuted earlier. Both defects are actually fixed, not papered over: I probed cap_lag_orders directly, outside the author's own harness, and an empty live-account list or a live account missing from the list now BLOCKS instead of silently falling back to share=1.0, with N sourced from load_accounts() across all brokers rather than the DNSE-filtered live_dnse_labels() — the 2x20%=40% ADV regression is prevented. The premise correction checks out at source: simulate_holistic_nav.py:1169-1171 treats liq==0 as falsy and fills uncapped, so the gate is a new conservative divergence rather than a fidelity patch, and the v2 docstring now says exactly that; the min_fill_pct citation is also fixed. Most decisively, I rebuilt the LAG candidate basket from raw artifacts with my own code and reproduced their headline measurement: 5,319 events / 790 tickers exactly, 12.84% liq<=0 against their 12.80%, 1.56% NaN exactly, and a separate BQ query confirms ticker_prune 2014+ has zero liq<=0 rows. The self-check passes (29 checks, exit 0; the '27/27' label is a stale hand count). One claim I do not accept: calling 14.8% an upper bound on funded deals is unsupported and probably backwards, since uncapped liq=0 names clear the fill queue in one session while capped names tie up capital for five, which over-represents them among funded deals. The code is sound and the payload is correctly honest that it is not production-ready pending the user's Phương án A decision — but that decision should be presented without the false comfort of an upper bound.", "": ""}

The bracket is correct but strongly skewed to its LOW end, and the finding does not say so.", "recommended_reruns": ["Do NOT quote 31.33% anywhere; if the bracket is cited, state explicitly that expectation sits near ~27.5%, not mid-range.", "Run the true lower bound: a third arm where blocked capital is NOT substituted (order dropped, cash stays in the LAG park) — that is the number that actually describes the live path, and it is currently unmeasured.", "Only after user approval of signal-tier filtering (exclude Volume_3M_P50<=0 at golive_recommend_v23.py / LAG signal build) does the engine's substitution behaviour become a faithful mirror — re-pin only then.", "Re-run both arms once data/bq_cache passes verification (ticker_prune 756,091 local vs 760,296 BQ still open) to confirm the delta is vintage-stable.", "Fix the pin command in the registry: BQ_LOCAL_CACHE=data/bq_cache, never =1 (silent fallback to live BQ), and require the '[BQ_LOCAL_CACHE] ready' line."], "summary": "The bug is real and I verified it against BigQuery, not just the logs: the control arm bought 142B VND of LAW, a stock whose median daily volume is literally zero on 98% of sessions — those fills could never have happened. The fix is surgical and default-off, the two arms are a clean same-vintage contemporaneous pair with 0 VND self-checks on both books, no look-ahead enters the gate, and I reproduced every figure in the mechanism block exactly from the cited CSVs. The finding's own headline caveat — that 31.33% is an UPPER BOUND, not a quotable number — is correct and is the reason this survives. Where it under-sells its own caveat: the +4.11pp is overwhelmingly the substitution assumption (avoided losses of -28.5B explain only ~8% of the +369.5B LAG-book gap), and since the live path leaves blocked capital idle rather than rolling it to the next candidate, the realistic outcome sits near the LOW end of [27.2%, 31.3%] rather than mid-range. Keep 27.84% as the pin, do not re-pin until the no-substitution arm is measured and the signal-tier decision is made."}

The finding discloses this precisely, calls 31.33% a ceiling, and keeps 27.84% as the pin — so it does not void the claim, but 31.33% must never be quoted standalone.", "recommended_reruns": ["Re-run both legs with BQ_LOCAL_CACHE=data/bq_cache once the cache returns to verified:true (ticker_prune 756,091 vs 760,296 corruption still open) to fix the absolute levels; the A/B delta should be re-measured then", "Run LIQ_ZERO_BLOCK=both to size the same fidelity defect on the BAL book, which the 'lag' scope leaves unmeasured (BAL currently still buys full size on any liq<=0 name)", "Save the dsr_pbo_annex stdout for the treat leg into data/liqzb_20260721/ — only annex_liqzb.py exists, no output log, so DSR 1.0000 / PBO 0.3713 / bootstrap 21.1%/-30.5% are currently untraceable", "Before acting on the upper bound: implement + A/B the Volume_3M_P50<=0 filter at the SIGNAL layer (golive_recommend_v23.py / LAG book) so backtest and live share the substitution semantics — flagged as needing separate user approval"], "summary": "This survived every attack I could mount. The cited engine line and the surgical default-off fix are exactly as described; both runs are real, contemporaneous (BAL leg byte-identical across legs proves same data vintage and correct scope), self-check 0 VND on all four book-legs, borrow 0, threads=1. I independently reproduced every headline to the quoted precision — 27.22/31.33 CAGR, both IS/OOS splits, both MaxDDs, and the complete mechanism decomposition (58 tickers, 12.8% of LAG turnover, -28.5B net, -1.11% vs +4.82% per round, 5,460->9,806 orders) — plus my own leave-one-out reproducing 13/13 positive with the same min/max years. No forward column is touched, the universe is a live BQ pull, and the change is binary rather than tuned. The one real weakness, that +4.11pp comes overwhelmingly from same-session capital substitution the live executor-layer gate will not deliver, is stated by the finding itself, which is why it keeps 27.84% as the official pin and reports [~27.2%; 31.3%] as the honest range. Untraced items are secondary: the DSR/PBO/bootstrap annex has no saved output, LIQ_ZERO_BLOCK=both was never run so the BAL-side defect is unmeasured, and absolute levels remain non-reproducible until the BQ cache is verified again."}

Second, and decisively, I measured the thing the finding asserted without measuring: real LAG concurrency in both A/B runs peaks at 16-17 and exceeds 12 on 19-32% of sessions, mean under 5. max_positions=12 is a leaky gate evaluated only at first fill against completed positions, so it does not bind the LAG book at 12 and is not the everyday constraint — capital is. The separately-reported live gap is real and correctly identified (MAX_POS=12 is BAL-only, at golive_recommend_v23.py:346; no LAG position cap anywhere on the live path), though its backtest counterpart is ~16-17, not the 12 the finding assumes.
